| development/creativeEngineering/src/main/resources/kubejs_startup/creativeengineering/changes/transition_items.js | 76 | ---- wire coils + coil blocks + pack components ---------------------------------------- Keep COIL_METALS in sync with server_scripts/metal/metal_base_materials.js. coil_<m>       : wire-coil-in-progress  — gray-tint of the wire coil's item sprite, taken from i.wire_coil_<m> (the dict has one key per id since 2026-10-27, so there is no i.coil_<m> to auto-derive from; the incomplete id stays kubejs:incomplete_coil_<m>). creativeengineering:* for the 9 pack coils, immersiveengineering:* for copper / steel / electrum. coil_block_<m> : coil-block-in-progress — the block's own side texture; the coil-block item model is a 3D block, so there is no flat item sprite to derive from. copper -> IE coil_lv, electrum -> IE coil_mv (placeholder until a creativeengineering:coil_electrum block exists); the other 10 (incl. steel) -> the pack's own coil block. | ---- wire coils + coil blocks + pack components ---------------------------------------- Keep COIL_METALS in sync with server_scripts/metal/metal_base_materials.js. coil_<m>: wire-coil-in-progress — gray-tint of the wire coil's item sprite, taken from i.wire_coil_<m> (the dict has one key per id, so there is no i.coil_<m> to auto-derive from; the incomplete id stays kubejs:incomplete_coil_<m>). creativeengineering:* for the 9 pack coils, immersiveengineering:* for copper / steel / electrum. coil_block_<m>: coil-block-in-progress — the block's own side texture; the coil-block item model is a 3D block, so there is no flat item sprite to derive from. copper -> IE coil_lv, electrum -> IE coil_mv (placeholder until a creativeengineering:coil_electrum block exists); the other 10 (incl. steel) -> the pack's own coil block. |

others: restriction) watch TYPE X Y Z R N EVERY TAG [REF]   sample like log N times, EVERY ticks apart; REF = @BOTNAME or x,y,z for the distance column assert TAG STAT OP V        compare a watch statistic: "[zhh3] assert TAG STAT=v OP V PASS/FAIL" seen TAG GOAL               did goal class GOAL run in any sample of TAG: "[zhh3] seen TAG GOAL yes/no" goals TYPE X Y Z R          the goal list of each entity with priorities (available goals, running ones marked) tally X Y Z R               "tally crows=N tamed=T carrying=C shoulder=S" for the crows in the box; crows on the shoulders of players in the box count too (S of them) target X Y Z R ITEM        CrowEntity.isFood(ITEM) for each crow (#creativedelight:crow_food): "[zhh3] isFood ITEM true/false wild=B" block X Y Z                 block id and properties poi X Y Z                   POI type at the position, or none items X Y Z R [clear]       item entities (id, count, Age, Invulnerable, Thrower present, PickupDelay), then "items total=N id xN ..." inv NAME [ITEM]             hotbar, main, offhand of a player; with ITEM also "invcount NAME ITEM n" throw NAME X Y Z ITEM N [SPREAD]   N item entities (1 item each) with player NAME as thrower, no pickup delay hit NAME X Y Z R [DMG]      player NAME hits the nearest crow to X Y Z (DamageSource.playerAttack, default 1) feed NAME X Y Z R           player NAME right-clicks every wild crow in the box once (Player.interactOn, main hand) interact NAME X Y Z R       player NAME right-clicks every crow in the box (wild or tame), main hand tame NAME X Y Z R           tames every crow in the box to player NAME (TamableAnimal.tame) sneak NAME on/off / sprint NAME on/off   set the flag directly; bots have no input loot X Y Z R ITEM [TICKS]   CrowLogic.holdLoot on every crow in the box (TICKS overrides the carry timer) leash NAME X Y Z R TYPE     leash every TYPE in the box to player NAME enttag TAG                  entity types in an entity type tag nests X Z R                 nests at the surface (tree tops, roofs, ground) in a loaded square: generated / placed counts, first 5 with biome kill X Y Z TYPE N LOOT      a fake player with an iron sword of Looting LOOT kills N fresh TYPE at X Y Z; sums the drops TYPE defaults to hearthandharvest:crow when written "crow" (the Hearth and Harvest crow since 2026-11-10; wariness is the entity's own field, NBT "Wariness"). | cfgdump                     every [salt] and [crow] value as the game reads it cfg KEY [true/false/D]      read a key, or live-set a boolean / chance key of [crow] or [salt] (ints: toml before boot) spawn TYPE N X Y Z [SPREAD] [noai]   summon N of TYPE around X Y Z (random within SPREAD horizontally) log TYPE X Y Z R [REF]      one line per entity within R of X Y Z, with wariness state and goals (crows: wariness; others: restriction) watch TYPE X Y Z R N EVERY TAG [REF]   sample like log N times, EVERY ticks apart; REF = @BOTNAME or x,y,z for the distance column assert TAG STAT OP V        compare a watch statistic: "[zhh3] assert TAG STAT=v OP V PASS/FAIL" seen TAG GOAL               did goal class GOAL run in any sample of TAG: "[zhh3] seen TAG GOAL yes/no" goals TYPE X Y Z R          the goal list of each entity with priorities (available goals, running ones marked) tally X Y Z R               "tally crows=N tamed=T carrying=C shoulder=S" for the crows in the box; crows on the shoulders of players in the box count too (S of them) target X Y Z R ITEM        CrowEntity.isFood(ITEM) for each crow (#creativedelight:crow_food): "[zhh3] isFood ITEM true/false wild=B" block X Y Z                 block id and properties poi X Y Z                   POI type at the position, or none items X Y Z R [clear]       item entities (id, count, Age, Invulnerable, Thrower present, PickupDelay), then "items total=N id xN ..." inv NAME [ITEM]             hotbar, main, offhand of a player; with ITEM also "invcount NAME ITEM n" throw NAME X Y Z ITEM N [SPREAD]   N item entities (1 item each) with player NAME as thrower, no pickup delay hit NAME X Y Z R [DMG]      player NAME hits the nearest crow to X Y Z (DamageSource.playerAttack, default 1) feed NAME X Y Z R           player NAME right-clicks every wild crow in the box once (Player.interactOn, main hand) interact NAME X Y Z R       player NAME right-clicks every crow in the box (wild or tame), main hand tame NAME X Y Z R           tames every crow in the box to player NAME (TamableAnimal.tame) sneak NAME on/off / sprint NAME on/off   set the flag directly; bots have no input loot X Y Z R ITEM [TICKS]   CrowLogic.holdLoot on every crow in the box (TICKS overrides the carry timer) leash NAME X Y Z R TYPE     leash every TYPE in the box to player NAME enttag TAG                  entity types in an entity type tag nests X Z R                 nests at the surface (tree tops, roofs, ground) in a loaded square: generated / placed counts, first 5 with biome kill X Y Z TYPE N LOOT      a fake player with an iron sword of Looting LOOT kills N fresh TYPE at X Y Z; sums the drops TYPE defaults to hearthandharvest:crow when written "crow" (the Hearth and Harvest crow; wariness is the entity's own field, NBT "Wariness"). |
